<file path="assets/js/main.js">
/* SIPN — Global UI interactions */
(() => {
    'use strict';

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initializeSIPNUI);

    function initializeSIPNUI() {
        const body = document.body;

        initializeThemeToggle();
        initializeProfileDropdown();
        initializeModalController();
        initializeResponsiveSidebar();

        function initializeThemeToggle() {
            const themeToggle = document.getElementById('themeToggleSidebar');
            const themeText = document.getElementById('themeText');
            let savedTheme = 'light';

            try {
                const storedTheme = window.localStorage.getItem('sipn_theme');
                if (storedTheme === 'dark' || storedTheme === 'light') {
                    savedTheme = storedTheme;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.warn('Preferensi tema tidak dapat dibaca.', error);
            }

            const applyTheme = (theme) => {
                body.setAttribute('data-theme', theme);

                if (themeText) {
                    themeText.textContent = theme === 'dark' ? 'Light Mode' : 'Dark Mode';
                }

                if (themeToggle) {
                    themeToggle.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(theme === 'dark'));
                    themeToggle.setAttribute(
                        'aria-label',
                        theme === 'dark' ? 'Beralih ke tema terang' : 'Beralih ke tema gelap'
                    );
                }
            };

            applyTheme(savedTheme);

            if (!themeToggle) return;

            themeToggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const currentTheme = body.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';
                const nextTheme = currentTheme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
                applyTheme(nextTheme);

                try {
                    window.localStorage.setItem('sipn_theme', nextTheme);
                } catch (error) {
                    console.warn('Preferensi tema tidak dapat disimpan.', error);
                }
            });
        }

        function initializeProfileDropdown() {
            const trigger = document.getElementById('profileTriggerBtn');
            const dropdown = document.getElementById('profileDropdown');
            if (!trigger || !dropdown) return;

            const setOpen = (isOpen) => {
                dropdown.classList.toggle('is-open', isOpen);
                dropdown.setAttribute('aria-hidden', String(!isOpen));
                trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(isOpen));
            };

            setOpen(false);

            trigger.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
                event.stopPropagation();
                const isOpen = trigger.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
                setOpen(!isOpen);
            });

            dropdown.addEventListener('click', (event) => event.stopPropagation());

            document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
                if (!trigger.contains(event.target) && !dropdown.contains(event.target)) {
                    setOpen(false);
                }
            });

            document.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
                if (event.key === 'Escape' && trigger.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true') {
                    setOpen(false);
                    trigger.focus();
                }
            });
        }

        function initializeModalController() {
            let activeModal = null;
            let returnFocusTo = null;
            let previousBodyOverflow = '';

            const getFocusableElements = (container) => {
                if (!container) return [];

                const selector = [
                    'a[href]',
                    'area[href]',
                    'button:not([disabled])',
                    'input:not([disabled]):not([type="hidden"])',
                    'select:not([disabled])',
                    'textarea:not([disabled])',
                    'iframe',
                    '[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
                ].join(',');

                return Array.from(container.querySelectorAll(selector)).filter((element) => {
                    return !element.hasAttribute('hidden') && element.getAttribute('aria-hidden') !== 'true';
                });
            };

            const openModal = (selector, trigger) => {
                let modal;
                try {
                    modal = document.querySelector(selector);
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error('Selector modal tidak valid:', selector, error);
                    return;
                }

                if (!modal || !modal.classList.contains('modal-overlay')) {
                    console.warn('Modal target tidak ditemukan:', selector);
                    return;
                }

                if (activeModal && activeModal !== modal) {
                    closeModal(activeModal, false);
                }

                returnFocusTo = trigger || document.activeElement;
                previousBodyOverflow = body.style.overflow;
                activeModal = modal;

                modal.classList.add('is-active');
                modal.setAttribute('role', 'dialog');
                modal.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
                modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
                if (!modal.hasAttribute('tabindex')) modal.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1');

                body.style.overflow = 'hidden';

                const focusable = getFocusableElements(modal);
                (focusable[0] || modal).focus();
            };

            const closeModal = (modal = activeModal, restoreFocus = true) => {
                if (!modal) return;

                modal.classList.remove('is-active');
                modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
                modal.removeAttribute('aria-modal');

                if (modal === activeModal) {
                    activeModal = null;
                    body.style.overflow = previousBodyOverflow;
                }

                if (restoreFocus && returnFocusTo && document.contains(returnFocusTo)) {
                    returnFocusTo.focus();
                }
                if (modal === activeModal || !activeModal) returnFocusTo = null;
            };

            document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
                const target = event.target instanceof Element ? event.target : event.target.parentElement;
                if (!target) return;

                const opener = target.closest('[data-modal-target]');
                if (opener) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    openModal(opener.getAttribute('data-modal-target'), opener);
                    return;
                }

                const closer = target.closest('[data-modal-close]');
                if (closer) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    closeModal(closer.closest('.modal-overlay'));
                    return;
                }

                if (activeModal && target === activeModal) {
                    closeModal(activeModal);
                }
            });

            document.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
                if (!activeModal) return;

                if (event.key === 'Escape') {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    closeModal(activeModal);
                    return;
                }

                if (event.key !== 'Tab') return;

                const focusable = getFocusableElements(activeModal);
                if (focusable.length === 0) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    activeModal.focus();
                    return;
                }

                const first = focusable[0];
                const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];

                if (event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === first || !activeModal.contains(document.activeElement))) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    last.focus();
                } else if (!event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === last || !activeModal.contains(document.activeElement))) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    first.focus();
                }
            });
        }

        function initializeResponsiveSidebar() {
            const sidebar = document.getElementById('appSidebar');
            const toggle = document.getElementById('sidebarToggle');
            const backdrop = document.getElementById('sidebarBackdrop');
            const mainContent = document.getElementById('appMainContent');

            if (!sidebar || !toggle || !backdrop || !mainContent) return;

            const mobileQuery = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 768px)');
            const tabletQuery = window.matchMedia('(min-width: 769px) and (max-width: 1199px)');

            const setToggleState = (expanded, label) => {
                toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(expanded));
                toggle.setAttribute('aria-label', label);
            };

            const closeMobileSidebar = (restoreFocus = false) => {
                body.classList.remove('sidebar-open');
                backdrop.classList.remove('is-visible');
                backdrop.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
                backdrop.tabIndex = -1;
                sidebar.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
                sidebar.inert = true;
                mainContent.inert = false;
                setToggleState(false, 'Buka menu navigasi');
                if (restoreFocus) toggle.focus();
            };

            const openMobileSidebar = () => {
                body.classList.add('sidebar-open');
                backdrop.classList.add('is-visible');
                backdrop.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
                backdrop.tabIndex = 0;
                sidebar.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
                sidebar.inert = false;
                mainContent.inert = true;
                setToggleState(true, 'Tutup menu navigasi');

                const firstLink = sidebar.querySelector('a.nav-link, button.nav-link');
                if (firstLink) firstLink.focus();
            };

            const syncViewportState = () => {
                if (mobileQuery.matches) {
                    body.classList.remove('sidebar-expanded');
                    const isOpen = body.classList.contains('sidebar-open');
                    sidebar.inert = !isOpen;
                    sidebar.setAttribute('aria-hidden', String(!isOpen));
                    mainContent.inert = isOpen;
                    backdrop.classList.toggle('is-visible', isOpen);
                    backdrop.setAttribute('aria-hidden', String(!isOpen));
                    backdrop.tabIndex = isOpen ? 0 : -1;
                    setToggleState(isOpen, isOpen ? 'Tutup menu navigasi' : 'Buka menu navigasi');
                    return;
                }

                body.classList.remove('sidebar-open');
                backdrop.classList.remove('is-visible');
                backdrop.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
                backdrop.tabIndex = -1;
                sidebar.inert = false;
                sidebar.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
                mainContent.inert = false;

                if (tabletQuery.matches) {
                    const isExpanded = body.classList.contains('sidebar-expanded');
                    setToggleState(isExpanded, isExpanded ? 'Ciutkan navigasi' : 'Perluas navigasi');
                } else {
                    body.classList.remove('sidebar-expanded');
                    setToggleState(false, 'Navigasi utama');
                }
            };

            toggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
                if (mobileQuery.matches) {
                    const isOpen = body.classList.contains('sidebar-open');
                    if (isOpen) closeMobileSidebar(true);
                    else openMobileSidebar();
                    return;
                }

                if (tabletQuery.matches) {
                    const isExpanded = body.classList.toggle('sidebar-expanded');
                    setToggleState(isExpanded, isExpanded ? 'Ciutkan navigasi' : 'Perluas navigasi');
                }
            });

            backdrop.addEventListener('click', () => closeMobileSidebar(true));

            sidebar.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
                const link = event.target instanceof Element ? event.target.closest('a.nav-link') : null;
                if (link && mobileQuery.matches) closeMobileSidebar(false);
            });

            document.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
                if (event.key === 'Escape') {
                    if (mobileQuery.matches && body.classList.contains('sidebar-open')) {
                        closeMobileSidebar(true);
                    } else if (tabletQuery.matches && body.classList.contains('sidebar-expanded')) {
                        body.classList.remove('sidebar-expanded');
                        setToggleState(false, 'Perluas navigasi');
                        toggle.focus();
                    }
                    return;
                }

                if (event.key !== 'Tab' || !mobileQuery.matches || !body.classList.contains('sidebar-open')) return;

                const focusable = Array.from(sidebar.querySelectorAll(
                    'a[href], button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
                )).filter((element) => !element.hasAttribute('hidden'));

                if (focusable.length === 0) return;
                const first = focusable[0];
                const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];

                if (event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === first || !sidebar.contains(document.activeElement))) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    last.focus();
                } else if (!event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === last || !sidebar.contains(document.activeElement))) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    first.focus();
                }
            });

            if (typeof mobileQuery.addEventListener === 'function') {
                mobileQuery.addEventListener('change', syncViewportState);
                tabletQuery.addEventListener('change', syncViewportState);
            } else {
                mobileQuery.addListener(syncViewportState);
                tabletQuery.addListener(syncViewportState);
            }

            syncViewportState();
        }
    }
})();
</file>

/* =========================================================
   AUTHENTICATION PAGES — SUBTLE HOVER INTERACTIONS
   Tidak ada animasi otomatis saat halaman dibuka.
   Efek hanya berjalan ketika elemen di-hover atau difokuskan.
   ========================================================= */

/* Tombol utama: sedikit terangkat dengan transisi halus */
.auth-page .auth-btn-submit {
  transition:
    background-color 180ms ease,
    box-shadow 180ms ease,
    transform 180ms ease;
  will-change: transform;
}

.auth-page .auth-btn-submit:hover {
  background: var(--primary-hover);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 7px 16px rgba(30, 58, 138, 0.18);
}

.auth-page .auth-btn-submit:hover i {
  transform: translateX(3px);
}

.auth-page .auth-btn-submit i {
  transition: transform 180ms ease;
}

.auth-page .auth-btn-submit:active {
  background: var(--primary-dark);
  transform: translateY(0);
  box-shadow: none;
}

/* Link: perubahan warna dan garis bawah tidak mendadak */
.auth-page a {
  transition: color 160ms ease, text-decoration-color 160ms ease;
}

.auth-page a:hover {
  color: var(--primary-hover) !important;
  text-decoration: underline !important;
}

/* Input: fokus lebih jelas, tanpa gerakan berlebihan */
.auth-page .auth-input-wrapper input,
.auth-page .auth-input-wrapper select {
  transition: border-color 160ms ease, box-shadow 160ms ease;
}

.auth-page .auth-input-wrapper input:focus,
.auth-page .auth-input-wrapper select:focus {
  border-color: var(--primary-dark);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(30, 58, 138, 0.10);
}

/* Fokus keyboard tetap terlihat */
.auth-page button:focus-visible,
.auth-page a:focus-visible,
.auth-page input:focus-visible,
.auth-page select:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--primary-hover);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

/* Hormati pengaturan pengurangan gerakan perangkat */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .auth-page .auth-btn-submit,
  .auth-page .auth-btn-submit i,
  .auth-page a,
  .auth-page .auth-input-wrapper input,
  .auth-page .auth-input-wrapper select {
    transition: none !important;
  }

  .auth-page .auth-btn-submit:hover,
  .auth-page .auth-btn-submit:active,

    transform: none !important;
  }
}

<?php
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}
require_once __DIR__ . '/config/koneksi_database.php';

// Jika pengguna SUDAH login, langsung lempar ke dashboard masing-masing
if (isset($_SESSION['role'])) {
    switch ($_SESSION['role']) {
        case 'admin':
            header("Location: " . BASE_URL . "admin/dashboard_admin.php");
            exit();
        case 'guru':
            header("Location: " . BASE_URL . "guru/dashboard_guru.php");
            exit();
        case 'siswa':
            header("Location: " . BASE_URL . "siswa/dashboard_siswa.php");
            exit();
    }
}

// Jika BELUM login, alihkan ke form login
header("Location: " . BASE_URL . "auth/form_login.php");
exit();
?>

<file path="auth/form_login.php">
<?php
session_start();
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';

if (isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    $role = $_SESSION['role'];
    header("Location: " . BASE_URL . $role . "/dashboard_" . $role . ".php");
    exit();
}

$error = '';

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $username = trim($_POST['username'] ?? '');
    $password = trim($_POST['password'] ?? '');

    if (empty($username) || empty($password)) {
        $error = 'Username dan Password wajib diisi!';
    } else {
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "SELECT id, username, password, role FROM users WHERE username = ?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "s", $username);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);

        if ($user = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
            if (password_verify($password, $user['password'])) {
                // Regenerasi ID session setelah autentikasi berhasil.
                session_regenerate_id(true);

                $_SESSION['user_id']  = $user['id'];
                $_SESSION['username'] = $user['username'];
                $_SESSION['role']     = $user['role'];
                $_SESSION['sipn_dashboard_entry'] = true;

                set_flash_message('success', 'Selamat datang kembali, ' . $user['username'] . '!');

                header("Location: " . BASE_URL . $user['role'] . "/dashboard_" . $user['role'] . ".php");
                exit();
            } else {
                $error = 'Password yang Anda masukkan salah!';
            }
        } else {
            $error = 'Username tidak ditemukan!';
        }
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
    }
}

<file path="auth/proses_logout.php">
<?php
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';

// Hapus semua variabel session
$_SESSION = array();

// Hapus cookie session jika ada
if (ini_get("session.use_cookies")) {
    $params = session_get_cookie_params();
    setcookie(session_name(), '', time() - 42000,
        $params["path"], $params["domain"],
        $params["secure"], $params["httponly"]
    );
}

// Hancurkan session
session_destroy();

// Mulai session baru untuk pesan notifikasi
session_start();
set_flash_message('info', 'Anda telah berhasil logout.');

header("Location: " . BASE_URL . "auth/form_login.php");
exit();
?>
</file>

<file path="auth/form_register_siswa.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $username = sanitize($_POST['username']);
    $password = password_hash($_POST['password'], PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
    $nis      = sanitize($_POST['nis']);
    $nama     = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
    $kelas    = sanitize($_POST['kelas']);
    $jk       = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);

    mysqli_begin_transaction($conn);
    try {
        $stmt1 = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO users (username, password, role) VALUES (?, ?, 'siswa')");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt1, "ss", $username, $password);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt1);
        $user_id = mysqli_insert_id($conn);

        $stmt2 = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO siswa (nis, nama, kelas, jenis_kelamin, user_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt2, "ssssi", $nis, $nama, $kelas, $jk, $user_id);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt2);

        mysqli_commit($conn);
        set_flash_message('success', 'Registrasi berhasil! Silakan login.');
        header('Location: ' . BASE_URL . 'auth/form_login.php');
        exit();
    } catch (Throwable $e) {
        mysqli_rollback($conn);
        set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal registrasi! NIS atau Username mungkin sudah terdaftar.');
    }
}

<file path="guru/data_nilai_siswa.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';
check_role(['guru']);

$id_user = $_SESSION['user_id'];
$query_guru = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama FROM guru WHERE user_id = '$id_user'");
$data_guru = mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_guru);
$id_guru = $data_guru['id'] ?? 0;

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    // 1. Aksi Simpan / Edit Nilai
    if (isset($_POST['simpan_nilai'])) {
        $siswa_id           = intval($_POST['siswa_id']);
        $mapel_id           = intval($_POST['mapel_id']);
        $nilai_kkm          = intval($_POST['nilai_kkm']);
        $nilai_pengetahuan  = intval($_POST['nilai_pengetahuan']);
        $nilai_keterampilan = intval($_POST['nilai_keterampilan']);

        $rata_rata = ($nilai_pengetahuan + $nilai_keterampilan) / 2;
        $keterangan = ($rata_rata >= $nilai_kkm) ? 'Tuntas' : 'Belum Tuntas';

        // Logika Upsert: Update jika sudah ada, Insert jika belum ada
        $cek_nilai = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM nilai_rapor WHERE siswa_id = '$siswa_id' AND mapel_id = '$mapel_id'");

        if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_nilai) > 0) {
            $stmt = $conn->prepare("UPDATE nilai_rapor SET nilai_kkm = ?, nilai_pengetahuan = ?, nilai_keterampilan = ?, Keterangan = ? WHERE siswa_id = ? AND mapel_id = ?");
            $stmt->bind_param("iiisii", $nilai_kkm, $nilai_pengetahuan, $nilai_keterampilan, $keterangan, $siswa_id, $mapel_id);
        } else {
            $stmt = $conn->prepare("INSERT INTO nilai_rapor (siswa_id, mapel_id, nilai_kkm, nilai_pengetahuan, nilai_keterampilan, Keterangan) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)");
            $stmt->bind_param("iiiiis", $siswa_id, $mapel_id, $nilai_kkm, $nilai_pengetahuan, $nilai_keterampilan, $keterangan);
        }

        if ($stmt->execute()) {
            set_flash_message('success', 'Data nilai rapor berhasil disimpan!');
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menyimpan nilai rapor.');
        }
        $stmt->close();

        header("Location: " . BASE_URL . "guru/data_nilai_siswa.php");
        exit;
    } 
    // 2. Aksi Hapus Nilai
    elseif (isset($_POST['aksi']) && $_POST['aksi'] === 'hapus_nilai') {
        $id_nilai = intval($_POST['id_nilai']);

        // Verifikasi pengaman: hapus nilai hanya jika mapel milik guru yang sedang login
        $stmt = $conn->prepare("
            DELETE nr FROM nilai_rapor nr
            JOIN mapel m ON nr.mapel_id = m.id
            WHERE nr.id = ? AND m.guru_id = ?
        ");
        $stmt->bind_param("ii", $id_nilai, $id_guru);

        if ($stmt->execute()) {
            set_flash_message('success', 'Data nilai rapor berhasil dihapus!');
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menghapus data nilai.');
        }
        $stmt->close();

        header("Location: " . BASE_URL . "guru/data_nilai_siswa.php");
        exit;
    }
}

$query_mapel = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, kode_mapel, nama_mapel FROM mapel WHERE guru_id = '$id_guru'");
$query_siswa = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nis, nama, kelas FROM siswa ORDER BY nama ASC");
$query_nilai = mysqli_query($conn, "
    SELECT nr.*, s.nama AS nama_siswa, s.nis, s.kelas, m.nama_mapel
    FROM nilai_rapor nr
    JOIN siswa s ON nr.siswa_id = s.id
    JOIN mapel m ON nr.mapel_id = m.id
    WHERE m.guru_id = '$id_guru'
    ORDER BY s.nama ASC
");

$mapel_list = [];
while ($m = mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_mapel)) { $mapel_list[] = $m; }

$siswa_list = [];
while ($s = mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_siswa)) { $siswa_list[] = $s; }

$nilai_list = [];
while ($n = mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_nilai)) { $nilai_list[] = $n; }

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';
?>

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';

check_role(['guru']);

$id_user = (int) ($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);

$query_guru = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM guru WHERE user_id = '$id_user'");
$guru = $query_guru ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_guru) : null;
$id_guru = (int) ($guru['id'] ?? 0);
$nama_guru = $guru['nama'] ?? ($_SESSION['username'] ?? 'Guru');
$nip = $guru['nip'] ?? '-';

$q_mapel = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM mapel WHERE guru_id = '$id_guru'");
$row_mapel = $q_mapel ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_mapel) : null;
$total_mapel = $q_mapel ? (int) ($row_mapel['total'] ?? 0) : null;

$q_siswa = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM siswa");
$row_siswa = $q_siswa ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_siswa) : null;
$total_siswa = $q_siswa ? (int) ($row_siswa['total'] ?? 0) : null;

$q_nilai_input = mysqli_query($conn, "
    SELECT COUNT(nr.id) AS total
    FROM nilai_rapor nr
    JOIN mapel m ON nr.mapel_id = m.id
    WHERE m.guru_id = '$id_guru'
");
$row_nilai = $q_nilai_input ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_nilai_input) : null;
$total_nilai_input = $q_nilai_input ? (int) ($row_nilai['total'] ?? 0) : null;

$query_mapel_list = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, kode_mapel, nama_mapel FROM mapel WHERE guru_id = '$id_guru' ORDER BY nama_mapel ASC");

$dashboard_query_error = !$query_guru || !$q_mapel || !$q_siswa || !$q_nilai_input || !$query_mapel_list;

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';
?>

<div class="sipn-role-dashboard sipn-teacher-dashboard">
    <section class="sipn-role-hero" aria-labelledby="teacherDashboardTitle">

            <span class="sipn-role-eyebrow">
                <i class="bi bi-mortarboard-fill" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                Portal Akademik Pendidik
            </span>
            <h1 id="teacherDashboardTitle">Selamat Datang, <?= htmlspecialchars($nama_guru, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8'); ?></h1>
            <p>Pantau mata pelajaran dan kelola nilai peserta didik dari satu halaman.</p>
            <div class="sipn-role-identity">
                <span><i class="bi bi-person-vcard" aria-hidden="true"></i> NIP: <?= htmlspecialchars($nip, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8'); ?></span>
                <span><i class="bi bi-person-badge" aria-hidden="true"></i> Akun Guru</span>
            </div>
        </div>
        <a class="sipn-role-hero__action" href="<?= BASE_URL; ?>guru/data_nilai_siswa.php">
            <i class="bi bi-pencil-square" aria-hidden="true"></i>
            <span>Kelola Nilai Siswa</span>
            <i class="bi bi-arrow-up-right" aria-hidden="true"></i>
        </a>
    </section>

    <?php if ($dashboard_query_error): ?>
        <div class="sipn-role-notice" role="status">
            <i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle-fill" aria-hidden="true"></i>
            <span>Sebagian data dashboard gagal dimuat. Periksa koneksi database atau struktur tabel terkait.</span>
        </div>
    <?php endif; ?>

    <section class="sipn-role-stats sipn-role-stats--three" aria-label="Ringkasan aktivitas guru">
        <article class="sipn-role-stat sipn-role-stat--blue">
            <span class="sipn-role-stat__label">Mata Pelajaran Diampu</span>
            <span class="sipn-role-stat__main">
                <strong class="sipn-role-stat__value"><?= $total_mapel !== null ? number_format($total_mapel, 0, ',', '.') : '—'; ?></strong>
                <span class="sipn-role-stat__icon" aria-hidden="true"><i class="bi bi-journal-bookmark-fill"></i></span>
            </span>
            <span class="sipn-role-stat__foot">Jumlah mata pelajaran yang terhubung dengan akun Anda</span>

            <span class="sipn-role-stat__main">
                <strong class="sipn-role-stat__value"><?= $total_siswa !== null ? number_format($total_siswa, 0, ',', '.') : '—'; ?></strong>
                <span class="sipn-role-stat__icon" aria-hidden="true"><i class="bi bi-people-fill"></i></span>
            </span>
            <span class="sipn-role-stat__foot">Jumlah seluruh peserta didik dalam sistem</span>

<file path="siswa/lihat_nilai_rapor.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';

check_role(['siswa']);

$user_id = (int) ($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);

// Ambil identitas siswa dari akun yang sedang aktif.
$query_siswa = mysqli_query(
    $conn,
    "SELECT id, nama, nis, kelas FROM siswa WHERE user_id = '$user_id' LIMIT 1"
);
$siswa = $query_siswa ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_siswa) : null;
$id_siswa = (int) ($siswa['id'] ?? 0);

// Ambil nilai rapor beserta mata pelajaran dan guru pengampu.
$query_nilai = mysqli_query($conn, "
    SELECT
        nr.nilai_kkm,
        nr.nilai_pengetahuan,
        nr.nilai_keterampilan,
        nr.Keterangan,
        m.kode_mapel,
        m.nama_mapel,
        g.nama AS nama_guru
    FROM nilai_rapor nr
    JOIN mapel m ON nr.mapel_id = m.id
    JOIN guru g ON m.guru_id = g.id
    WHERE nr.siswa_id = '$id_siswa'
    ORDER BY m.nama_mapel ASC
");

$total_mapel = $query_nilai ? mysqli_num_rows($query_nilai) : 0;

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';
?>

<div class="sipn-rapor-page">
    <section class="sipn-rapor-hero no-print" aria-labelledby="raporPageTitle">
        <div class="sipn-rapor-hero__copy">
            <span class="sipn-rapor-eyebrow">
                <i class="bi bi-mortarboard-fill" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                Portal Akademik Peserta Didik
            </span>
            <h1 id="raporPageTitle">Nilai Rapor</h1>
            <p>Pantau hasil belajar dan capaian kompetensi dari seluruh mata pelajaran.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="sipn-rapor-hero__actions">
            <a class="sipn-rapor-button sipn-rapor-button--secondary" href="<?= BASE_URL; ?>siswa/dashboard_siswa.php">
                <i class="bi bi-arrow-left" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                <span>Kembali ke Beranda</span>
            </a>
            <button type="button" onclick="window.print()" class="sipn-rapor-button sipn-rapor-button--primary">
                <i class="bi bi-printer" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                <span>Cetak Rapor</span>
            </button>
        </div>
    </section>

    <?php if (!$query_siswa || !$query_nilai): ?>
        <div class="sipn-rapor-alert" role="alert">
            <i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle-fill" aria-hidden="true"></i>
            <div>
                <strong>Data rapor belum dapat dimuat.</strong>
                <span>Terjadi kendala saat mengambil data. Silakan muat ulang halaman atau hubungi administrator.</span>
            </div>
        </div>

                                            <?= htmlspecialchars($keterangan, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8'); ?>
                                        </span>
                                    </td>

                            <?php
                                $urutan_baris++;
                            endwhile;

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';

check_role(['siswa']);

// Ambil data siswa berdasarkan akun yang sedang login.
$query_siswa = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM siswa WHERE user_id = '$user_id'");
$siswa = $query_siswa ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_siswa) : null;

if (!$siswa) {
    die("Data profil siswa tidak ditemukan. Hubungi Administrator.");
}

$id_siswa   = (int) $siswa['id'];
$nama_siswa = $siswa['nama'];
$nis        = $siswa['nis'];
$kelas      = $siswa['kelas'];

// Hitung statistik berdasarkan data akademik siswa.
$query_stat = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_nilai FROM nilai_rapor WHERE siswa_id = '$id_siswa'");
$stat = $query_stat ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_stat) : null;
$total_nilai = $query_stat ? (int) ($stat['total_nilai'] ?? 0) : null;

$query_mapel = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_mapel FROM mengajar WHERE siswa_id = '$id_siswa'");
$stat_mapel = $query_mapel ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_mapel) : null;
$total_mapel = $query_mapel ? (int) ($stat_mapel['total_mapel'] ?? 0) : null;

$query_nilai_list = mysqli_query($conn, "
    SELECT nr.*, m.kode_mapel, m.nama_mapel, g.nama AS nama_guru
    FROM nilai_rapor nr
    JOIN mapel m ON nr.mapel_id = m.id
    JOIN guru g ON m.guru_id = g.id
    WHERE nr.siswa_id = '$id_siswa'
    ORDER BY m.nama_mapel ASC
");

$dashboard_query_error = !$query_siswa || !$query_stat || !$query_mapel || !$query_nilai_list;

<file path="profile.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/includes/helper_cek_login.php';

if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$user_id = $_SESSION['user_id'];
$role = $_SESSION['role'];

// 1. Ambil Data Gabungan (Users + Guru/Siswa)
$query_user = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT username, role, foto FROM users WHERE id = '$user_id'");
$data_user = mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_user);

$nama_lengkap = $data_user['username'];
$identitas_id = '';
$jenis_kelamin = '';
$kelas = '';

if ($role === 'guru') {
    $q_detail = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT nip, nama, jenis_kelamin FROM guru WHERE user_id = '$user_id'");
    if ($d = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_detail)) {
        $nama_lengkap = $d['nama'];
        $identitas_id = $d['nip'];
        $jenis_kelamin = $d['jenis_kelamin'];
    }
} elseif ($role === 'siswa') {
    $q_detail = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT nis, nama, kelas, jenis_kelamin FROM siswa WHERE user_id = '$user_id'");
    if ($d = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_detail)) {
        $nama_lengkap = $d['nama'];
        $identitas_id = $d['nis'];
        $kelas = $d['kelas'];
        $jenis_kelamin = $d['jenis_kelamin'];
    }
} elseif ($role === 'admin') {
    $nama_lengkap = 'Administrator Sistem';
}

// 2. Proses Update Informasi Menyeluruh
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['update_informasi'])) {
    $username_baru = sanitize($_POST['username']);
    $password_baru = trim($_POST['password']);
    
    mysqli_begin_transaction($conn);
    try {
        // A. Update tabel users
        $q_user_update = "UPDATE users SET username = '$username_baru'";
        if (!empty($password_baru)) {
            $password_hash = password_hash($password_baru, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
            $q_user_update .= ", password = '$password_hash'";
        }
        $q_user_update .= " WHERE id = '$user_id'";
        mysqli_query($conn, $q_user_update);
        
        // B. Update tabel spesifik sesuai role
        if ($role === 'guru') {
            $nip = sanitize($_POST['nip']);
            $nama = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
            $jk = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE guru SET nip = '$nip', nama = '$nama', jenis_kelamin = '$jk' WHERE user_id = '$user_id'");
        } elseif ($role === 'siswa') {
            $nis = sanitize($_POST['nis']);
            $nama = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
            $kls = sanitize($_POST['kelas']);
            $jk = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE siswa SET nis = '$nis', nama = '$nama', kelas = '$kls', jenis_kelamin = '$jk' WHERE user_id = '$user_id'");
        }

        mysqli_commit($conn);
        $_SESSION['username'] = $username_baru; // Update session
        set_flash_message('success', 'Informasi profil berhasil diperbarui secara menyeluruh!');
    } catch (Exception $e) {
        mysqli_rollback($conn);
        set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal memperbarui profil: ' . $e->getMessage());
    }
    
    header("Location: profile.php");
    exit;
}

// 3. Proses Update Foto (Upload Asli ke Direktori & Database)
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['update_foto'])) {
    if (isset($_FILES['foto_profil']) && $_FILES['foto_profil']['error'] === 0) {
        $file = $_FILES['foto_profil'];
        $nama_file = $file['name'];
        $ukuran_file = $file['size'];
        $tmp_name = $file['tmp_name'];
        
        $ekstensi_diperbolehkan = ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png'];
        $ekstensi = strtolower(pathinfo($nama_file, PATHINFO_EXTENSION));
        
        // Cek ekstensi file
        if (in_array($ekstensi, $ekstensi_diperbolehkan)) {
            // Cek ukuran file (Maksimal 2MB = 2097152 bytes)
            if ($ukuran_file <= 2097152) {
                // Buat nama file unik agar tidak bentrok
                $nama_file_baru = 'profil_' . $role . '_' . $user_id . '_' . time() . '.' . $ekstensi;
                
                // Tentukan lokasi folder penyimpanan (Otomatis dibuat jika belum ada)
                $direktori = __DIR__ . '/assets/img/profil/';
                if (!is_dir($direktori)) {
                    mkdir($direktori, 0777, true);
                }
                
                $path_simpan = $direktori . $nama_file_baru;
                
                // Pindahkan file dari memori sementara ke folder tujuan
                if (move_uploaded_file($tmp_name, $path_simpan)) {
                    
                    // (Opsional & Aman): Hapus foto lama di folder agar server tidak bengkak
                    if (!empty($data_user['foto']) && file_exists($direktori . $data_user['foto'])) {
                        unlink($direktori . $data_user['foto']);
                    }
                    
                    // Simpan nama file baru ke database
                    $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE users SET foto = ? WHERE id = ?");
                    mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "si", $nama_file_baru, $user_id);
                    
                    if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
                        set_flash_message('success', 'Foto profil berhasil diperbarui!');
                    } else {
                        set_flash_message('danger', 'File terunggah, tapi gagal menyimpan ke database.');
                    }
                } else {
                    set_flash_message('danger', 'Sistem gagal memindahkan file foto ke direktori server.');
                }
            } else {
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal! Ukuran foto terlalu besar (Maksimal 2MB).');
            }
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal! Format file tidak didukung. Gunakan JPG atau PNG.');
        }
    } else {
        set_flash_message('danger', 'Terjadi kesalahan atau tidak ada file yang dipilih.');
    }
    
    header("Location: profile.php");
    exit;
}
require_once __DIR__ . '/includes/template_header.php';
?>

<div class="profile-container-centered">
    
    <!-- Profil Card Berada di Tengah -->
    <div class="profile-card-centered">
        
        <!-- Bagian Atas: Avatar, Judul, Tombol Edit -->
        <div class="profile-header-centered">
            
            <button class="btn-primary-outline btn-edit-profile-absolute" data-modal-target="#modalOpsiEdit">
                <i class="bi bi-pencil-square"></i> Edit Profil

<!-- SCRIPT LIVE PREVIEW FOTO -->
<script>
function previewFoto(input) {
    const kontenAwal = document.getElementById('dropzoneContentAwal');
    const wadahPreview = document.getElementById('wadahPreview');
    const gambarPreview = document.getElementById('gambarPreview');
    const badgeFile = document.getElementById('badgeNamaFile');
    const teksNamaFile = document.getElementById('teksNamaFile');

    if (input.files && input.files[0]) {
        const reader = new FileReader();
        
        reader.onload = function(e) {
            // Sembunyikan ikon awan dan tampilkan foto
            kontenAwal.style.display = 'none';
            wadahPreview.style.display = 'block';
            gambarPreview.src = e.target.result;
            
            // Tampilkan badge nama file
            badgeFile.style.display = 'inline-flex';
            teksNamaFile.textContent = input.files[0].name;
        }
        
        reader.readAsDataURL(input.files[0]);
    } else {
        // Kembalikan ke tampilan awal jika pengguna batal memilih file
        kontenAwal.style.display = 'block';
        wadahPreview.style.display = 'none';
        badgeFile.style.display = 'none';
        gambarPreview.src = "";
    }
}
</script>

</file>

<?php

check_role(['admin']);

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['aksi'])) {
    $aksi = $_POST['aksi'];

    if ($aksi === 'tambah') {
        $mapel = (int)$_POST['mapel_id'];
        $siswa = (int)$_POST['siswa_id'];

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO mengajar (mapel_id, siswa_id) VALUES (?, ?)");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ii", $mapel, $siswa);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt) ? set_flash_message('success', 'Penugasan mengajar ditambahkan!') : set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menyimpan data.');
    } 
    elseif ($aksi === 'hapus') {
        $id_mengajar = (int)$_POST['id_mengajar'];
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "DELETE FROM mengajar WHERE id=?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $id_mengajar);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt) ? set_flash_message('success', 'Penugasan dihapus!') : set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menghapus.');
    }
    header("Location: data_mengajar.php");
    exit();
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';

$query = "
    SELECT mg.id, m.nama_mapel, m.kode_mapel, g.nama as nama_guru, s.nama as nama_siswa, s.kelas 
    FROM mengajar mg
    JOIN mapel m ON mg.mapel_id = m.id
    JOIN guru g ON m.guru_id = g.id
    JOIN siswa s ON mg.siswa_id = s.id
    ORDER BY m.nama_mapel ASC, s.kelas ASC, s.nama ASC
";
$result = mysqli_query($conn, $query);

$q_mapel = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama_mapel, kode_mapel FROM mapel ORDER BY nama_mapel ASC");
while ($m = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_mapel)) { 
    $opt_mapel .= "<option value='{$m['id']}'>[{$m['kode_mapel']}] {$m['nama_mapel']}</option>"; 
}

$opt_siswa = "";
$q_siswa = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama, kelas FROM siswa ORDER BY kelas ASC, nama ASC");
while ($s = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_siswa)) { 
    $opt_siswa .= "<option value='{$s['id']}'>{$s['nama']} - Kelas {$s['kelas']}</option>"; 
}

<file path="admin/data_siswa.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';
check_role(['admin']);

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['aksi'])) {
    $aksi = $_POST['aksi'];

    if ($aksi === 'tambah') {
        $nis      = sanitize($_POST['nis']);
        $nama     = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
        $kelas    = sanitize($_POST['kelas']);
        $jk       = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
        $username = sanitize($_POST['username']);
        $password = password_hash($_POST['password'], PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
        $role     = 'siswa';

        $cek = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username='$username' UNION SELECT id FROM siswa WHERE nis='$nis'");
        if (mysqli_num_rows($cek) > 0) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal! Username atau NIS sudah terdaftar.');
        } else {
            mysqli_begin_transaction($conn);
            try {
                $stmt1 = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO users (username, password, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?)");
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt1, "sss", $username, $password, $role);
                mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt1);
                $user_id = mysqli_insert_id($conn);

                $stmt2 = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO siswa (nis, nama, kelas, jenis_kelamin, user_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)");
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt2, "ssssi", $nis, $nama, $kelas, $jk, $user_id);
                mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt2);

                mysqli_commit($conn);
                set_flash_message('success', 'Data Siswa dan Akun berhasil ditambahkan!');
            } catch (Exception $e) {
                mysqli_rollback($conn);
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Terjadi kesalahan sistem: ' . $e->getMessage());
            }
        }
    } 
    elseif ($aksi === 'edit') {
        $id_siswa = (int)$_POST['id_siswa'];
        $nis      = sanitize($_POST['nis']);
        $nama     = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
        $kelas    = sanitize($_POST['kelas']);
        $jk       = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE siswa SET nis=?, nama=?, kelas=?, jenis_kelamin=? WHERE id=?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ssssi", $nis, $nama, $kelas, $jk, $id_siswa);
        
        if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
            set_flash_message('success', 'Data Siswa berhasil diperbarui!');
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal memperbarui data Siswa.');
        }
    }
    elseif ($aksi === 'hapus') {
        $user_id = (int)$_POST['user_id'];
        
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "DELETE FROM users WHERE id=?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $user_id);
        
        if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
            set_flash_message('success', 'Data Siswa beserta akun berhasil dihapus!');
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menghapus data.');
        }
    }
    header("Location: data_siswa.php");
    exit();
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';

$query = "SELECT s.*, u.username FROM siswa s JOIN users u ON s.user_id = u.id ORDER BY s.kelas ASC, s.nama ASC";
$result = mysqli_query($conn, $query);
?>

    <h2><i class="bi bi-person-badge"></i> Data Siswa</h2>

<file path="admin/data_guru.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';
check_role(['admin']);

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['aksi'])) {
    $aksi = $_POST['aksi'];

    if ($aksi === 'tambah') {
        $nip      = sanitize($_POST['nip']);
        $nama     = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
        $jk       = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
        $username = sanitize($_POST['username']);
        $password = password_hash($_POST['password'], PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
        $role     = 'guru';

        $cek = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username='$username' UNION SELECT id FROM guru WHERE nip='$nip'");
        if (mysqli_num_rows($cek) > 0) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal! Username atau NIP sudah terdaftar.');
        } else {
            mysqli_begin_transaction($conn);
            try {
                $stmt1 = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO users (username, password, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?)");
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt1, "sss", $username, $password, $role);
                mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt1);
                $user_id = mysqli_insert_id($conn);

                $stmt2 = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO guru (nip, nama, jenis_kelamin, user_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)");
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt2, "sssi", $nip, $nama, $jk, $user_id);
                mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt2);

                mysqli_commit($conn);
                set_flash_message('success', 'Data Guru dan Akun berhasil ditambahkan!');
            } catch (Exception $e) {
                mysqli_rollback($conn);
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Terjadi kesalahan sistem: ' . $e->getMessage());
            }
        }
    } 
    elseif ($aksi === 'edit') {
        $id_guru = (int)$_POST['id_guru'];
        $nip     = sanitize($_POST['nip']);
        $nama    = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
        $jk      = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE guru SET nip=?, nama=?, jenis_kelamin=? WHERE id=?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "sssi", $nip, $nama, $jk, $id_guru);
        
        if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
            set_flash_message('success', 'Data Guru berhasil diperbarui!');
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal memperbarui data Guru.');
        }
    }
    elseif ($aksi === 'hapus') {
        $user_id = (int)$_POST['user_id'];
        
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "DELETE FROM users WHERE id=?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $user_id);
        
        if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
            set_flash_message('success', 'Data Guru beserta akun berhasil dihapus!');
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menghapus data.');
        }
    }

    header("Location: data_guru.php");
    exit();
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';

$query = "SELECT g.*, u.username FROM guru g JOIN users u ON g.user_id = u.id ORDER BY g.nama ASC";
$result = mysqli_query($conn, $query);

$teachers = [];
while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
    $teachers[] = $row;
}
?>

<!-- Header Halaman & Tombol Tambah -->
<div class="page-header-action" style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 1rem;">
    <div>
        <h2 style="font-size: 1.25rem; font-weight: 800; color: var(--text-dark); display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.5rem; margin: 0;">
            <i class="bi bi-person-workspace text-primary"></i> Manajemen Data Guru
        </h2>
        <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin-top: 0.2rem; margin-bottom: 0;">Pusat pengelolaan data pendidik dan kredensial akun pengajar.</p>
    </div>
    <button type="button" class="btn-primary-custom" data-modal-target="#modalTambah">
        <i class="bi bi-plus-lg"></i> Tambah Guru Baru
    </button>
</div>

<!-- Tampilan Tabel Data Guru Full-Width -->
<div class="card-panel">
    <div class="table-responsive">
        <table class="data-table">
            <thead>
                <tr>
                    <th style="width: 5%;">No</th>
                    <th style="width: 18%;">NIP</th>
                    <th style="width: 32%;">Nama Guru & Gelar</th>
                    <th style="width: 12%;">Jenis Kelamin</th>
                    <th style="width: 18%;">Username Akun</th>
                    <th style="width: 15%;" class="text-center">Aksi</th>
                </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
                <?php if (count($teachers) > 0): ?>
                    <?php $no = 1; foreach ($teachers as $row): ?>
                    <tr>
                        <td><strong><?= $no++; ?></strong></td>
                        <td><?= htmlspecialchars($row['nip']); ?></td>
                        <td><strong><?= htmlspecialchars($row['nama']); ?></strong></td>
                        <td>
                            <span class="badge badge-primary">
                                <?= $row['jenis_kelamin'] === 'L' ? 'Laki-laki' : 'Perempuan'; ?>
                            </span>
                        </td>
                        <td><span class="badge badge-secondary"><?= htmlspecialchars($row['username']); ?></span></td>
                        <td class="text-center">
                            <div class="table-action-btn" style="display: inline-flex; justify-content: center; gap: 0.35rem;">
                                <button type="button" class= "btn btn-sm btn-warning" data-modal-target="#modalEdit<?= $row['id']; ?>" title="Edit Data">
                                    <i class="bi bi-pencil"></i>
                                </button>
                                <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" data-modal-target="#modalHapus<?= $row['id']; ?>" title="Hapus Data">
                                    <i class="bi bi-trash"></i>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                    <?php endforeach; ?>
                <?php else: ?>
                    <tr>
                        <td colspan="6" class="text-center text-muted" style="text-align: center; padding: 2rem;">Belum ada data guru terdaftar.</td>
                    </tr>
                <?php endif; ?>
            </tbody>
        </table>
    </div>
</div>

<!-- ================= SEMUA MODAL DI LUAR TABEL ================= -->
<?php foreach ($teachers as $row): ?>

<!-- Modal Edit Guru -->

    <div class="modal-box">
        <form method="POST">

            </div>

                <input type="hidden" name="id_guru" value="<?= $row['id']; ?>">
                
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label class="form-label">NIP</label>
                    <input type="text" class="form-control" name="nip" value="<?= htmlspecialchars($row['nip']); ?>" required>
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label class="form-label">Nama Lengkap & Gelar</label>
                    <input type="text" class="form-control" name="nama" value="<?= htmlspecialchars($row['nama']); ?>" required>
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label class="form-label">Jenis Kelamin</label>
                    <select class="form-select" name="jenis_kelamin" required>

                        <option value="P" <?= $row['jenis_kelamin'] == 'P' ? 'selected' : ''; ?>>Perempuan</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-footer">
                <button type="button" class="btn-secondary" data-modal-close>Batal</button>
                <button type="submit" class="btn-primary-custom">Simpan Perubahan</button>
            </div>
        </form>
    </div>

<!-- Modal Hapus Guru -->

            </div>
            <div class="modal-body">
                <input type="hidden" name="aksi" value="hapus">
                <input type="hidden" name="user_id" value="<?= $row['user_id']; ?>">

                <p style="font-size: 0.78rem; color: #dc2626; margin-top: 0.5rem;">Tindakan ini juga menghapus akun login terkait secara permanen.</p>
            </div>

<!-- Modal Tambah Guru & Akun -->
<div class="modal-overlay" id="modalTambah">

<file path="admin/data_user.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';
check_role(['admin']);

$logged_in_user_id = $_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0;

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['aksi'])) {
    $aksi = $_POST['aksi'];

    if ($aksi === 'reset_password') {
        $id_user   = (int)$_POST['id_user'];
        $pass_baru = password_hash($_POST['password_baru'], PASSWORD_DEFAULT);

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE users SET password = ? WHERE id = ?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "si", $pass_baru, $id_user);
        
        if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
            set_flash_message('success', 'Password pengguna berhasil diperbarui!');
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal memperbarui password.');
        }
    } 
    elseif ($aksi === 'edit_user') {
        $id_user  = (int)$_POST['id_user'];
        $username = sanitize($_POST['username']);
        $role     = sanitize($_POST['role']);

        $stmt_cek = mysqli_prepare($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ? AND id != ?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt_cek, "si", $username, $id_user);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt_cek);
        $res_cek = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt_cek);

        if (mysqli_num_rows($res_cek) > 0) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal! Username tersebut sudah digunakan oleh akun lain.');
        } else {
            $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE users SET username = ?, role = ? WHERE id = ?");
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ssi", $username, $role, $id_user);
            
            if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
                if ($id_user === $logged_in_user_id) {
                    $_SESSION['username'] = $username;
                    $_SESSION['role']     = $role;
                }
                set_flash_message('success', 'Data akun user berhasil diperbarui!');
            } else {
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal memperbarui data user.');
            }
        }
    }
    elseif ($aksi === 'hapus_user') {
        $id_user = (int)$_POST['id_user'];

        if ($id_user === $logged_in_user_id) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal! Anda tidak dapat menghapus akun Anda sendiri yang sedang aktif.');
        } else {
            $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "DELETE FROM users WHERE id = ?");
            mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $id_user);
            
            if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
                set_flash_message('success', 'Akun user berhasil dihapus!');
            } else {
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menghapus user.');
            }
        }
    }

    header("Location: data_user.php");
    exit();
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';

$query = "SELECT id, username, role FROM users ORDER BY role ASC, username ASC";
$result = mysqli_query($conn, $query);

$users = [];
while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
    $users[] = $row;
}
?>

<div class="page-header-action" style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 1.5rem;">
    <div>
        <h2 style="font-size: 1.25rem; font-weight: 800; color: var(--text-dark); display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.5rem; margin: 0;">
            <i class="bi bi-people text-primary"></i> Kelola Akun User
        </h2>
        <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin-top: 0.2rem; margin-bottom: 0;">Kelola hak akses role, kredensial login, dan reset kata sandi pengguna.</p>
    </div>
</div>

<div class="card-panel">
    <div class="table-responsive">
        <table class="data-table">
            <thead>
                <tr>
                    <th style="width: 5%;">No</th>
                    <th style="width: 30%;">Username</th>
                    <th style="width: 25%;">Role Hak Akses</th>
                    <th style="width: 40%;" class="text-center">Aksi Pengelolaan</th>
                </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
                <?php if (count($users) > 0): ?>
                    <?php $no = 1; foreach ($users as $row): ?>
                    <tr>
                        <td><?= $no++; ?></td>
                        <td>
                            <strong><?= htmlspecialchars($row['username']); ?></strong>
                            <?php if ($row['id'] === $logged_in_user_id): ?>
                                <span style="font-size: 0.7rem; color: #166534; background: #dcfce7; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 4px; margin-left: 0.4rem; font-weight: 700;">(Anda)</span>
                            <?php endif; ?>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <span class="badge badge-<?= $row['role'] === 'admin' ? 'danger' : ($row['role'] === 'guru' ? 'primary' : 'success'); ?>">
                                <?= strtoupper($row['role']); ?>
                            </span>
                        </td>
                        <td class="text-center">
                            <!-- Wrapper Flexbox agar tombol sejajar rapi ke samping -->
                            <div style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: 0.4rem; flex-wrap: nowrap;">
                               <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-secondary" data-modal-target="#modalEdit<?= $row['id']; ?>" title="Edit User">
                                    <i class="bi bi-key"></i> Edit
                                <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-warning" data-modal-target="#modalReset<?= $row['id']; ?>" title="Reset Password">
                                    <i class="bi bi-key"></i> Reset
                                </button>
                                <?php if ($row['id'] !== $logged_in_user_id): ?>
                                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" data-modal-target="#modalHapus<?= $row['id']; ?>" title="Hapus User">
                                        <i class="bi bi-trash"></i> Hapus
                                    </button>
                                <?php endif; ?>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                    <?php endforeach; ?>
                <?php else: ?>
                    <tr>
                        <td colspan="4" class="text-center" style="text-align: center; padding: 2rem;">Belum ada data user.</td>
                    </tr>
                <?php endif; ?>
            </tbody>
        </table>
    </div>
</div>

<!-- ================= MODAL DI LUAR TABEL ================= -->
<?php foreach ($users as $row): ?>

<!-- 1. Modal Edit User & Role -->
<div class="modal-overlay" id="modalEdit<?= $row['id']; ?>">
    <div class="modal-box">
        <form method="POST">
            <div class="modal-header header-primary">
                <h5>Edit Akun User - <?= htmlspecialchars($row['username']); ?></h5>
                <button type="button" class="modal-close-btn" data-modal-close>&times;</button>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-body">
                <input type="hidden" name="aksi" value="edit_user">
                <input type="hidden" name="id_user" value="<?= $row['id']; ?>">
                
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label class="form-label">Username</label>
                    <input type="text" class="form-control" name="username" value="<?= htmlspecialchars($row['username']); ?>" required>
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label class="form-label">Role Akses</label>
                    <select class="form-select" name="role" required>
                        <option value="admin" <?= $row['role'] === 'admin' ? 'selected' : ''; ?>>ADMIN</option>
                        <option value="guru" <?= $row['role'] === 'guru' ? 'selected' : ''; ?>>GURU</option>
                        <option value="siswa" <?= $row['role'] === 'siswa' ? 'selected' : ''; ?>>SISWA</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-footer">
                <button type="button" class="btn-secondary" data-modal-close>Batal</button>
                <button type="submit" class="btn-primary-custom">Simpan Perubahan</button>
            </div>
        </form>
    </div>
</div>

<!-- 2. Modal Reset Password -->
<div class="modal-overlay" id="modalReset<?= $row['id']; ?>">
    <div class="modal-box">
        <form method="POST">
            <div class="modal-header header-warning">
                <h5>Reset Password - <?= htmlspecialchars($row['username']); ?></h5>
                <button type="button" class="modal-close-btn" data-modal-close>&times;</button>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-body">
                <input type="hidden" name="aksi" value="reset_password">
                <input type="hidden" name="id_user" value="<?= $row['id']; ?>">
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label class="form-label">Password Baru</label>
                    <input type="password" class="form-control" name="password_baru" required minlength="4" placeholder="Masukkan password baru...">
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-footer">
                <button type="button" class="btn-secondary" data-modal-close>Batal</button>
                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-warning" style="background: #d97706; color: #fff; border: none; padding: 0.6rem 1.2rem; border-radius: 8px; font-weight: 700; cursor: pointer;">Simpan Password</button>
            </div>
        </form>
    </div>
</div>

<!-- 3. Modal Hapus User -->
<?php if ($row['id'] !== $logged_in_user_id): ?>
<div class="modal-overlay" id="modalHapus<?= $row['id']; ?>">
    <div class="modal-box">
        <form method="POST">
            <div class="modal-header header-danger">
                <h5>Konfirmasi Hapus Akun</h5>
                <button type="button" class="modal-close-btn" data-modal-close>&times;</button>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-body">
                <input type="hidden" name="aksi" value="hapus_user">
                <input type="hidden" name="id_user" value="<?= $row['id']; ?>">
                <p>Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus akun user <strong><?= htmlspecialchars($row['username']); ?></strong>?</p>
                <p style="font-size: 0.78rem; color: #dc2626; margin-top: 0.5rem; margin-bottom: 0;">
                    <i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle-fill"></i> Peringatan: Menghapus akun ini juga dapat menghapus data profil yang terhubung.
                </p>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-footer">
                <button type="button" class="btn-secondary" data-modal-close>Batal</button>
                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-danger" style="background: #dc2626; color: #fff; border: none; padding: 0.6rem 1.2rem; border-radius: 8px; font-weight: 700; cursor: pointer;">Ya, Hapus</button>
            </div>
        </form>
    </div>
</div>
<?php endif; ?>

<?php endforeach; ?>

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';
check_role(['admin']);

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['aksi'])) {
    $aksi = $_POST['aksi'];

    if ($aksi === 'tambah') {
        $kode = sanitize($_POST['kode_mapel']);
        $nama = sanitize($_POST['nama_mapel']);
        $guru = (int)$_POST['guru_id'];

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO mapel (kode_mapel, nama_mapel, guru_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?)");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ssi", $kode, $nama, $guru);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt) ? set_flash_message('success', 'Mapel berhasil ditambahkan!') : set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menambah mapel.');
    } 
    elseif ($aksi === 'edit') {
        $id_mapel = (int)$_POST['id_mapel'];
        $kode     = sanitize($_POST['kode_mapel']);
        $nama     = sanitize($_POST['nama_mapel']);
        $guru     = (int)$_POST['guru_id'];

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE mapel SET kode_mapel=?, nama_mapel=?, guru_id=? WHERE id=?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ssii", $kode, $nama, $guru, $id_mapel);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt) ? set_flash_message('success', 'Mapel berhasil diperbarui!') : set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal memperbarui.');
    }
    elseif ($aksi === 'hapus') {
        $id_mapel = (int)$_POST['id_mapel'];
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "DELETE FROM mapel WHERE id=?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $id_mapel);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt) ? set_flash_message('success', 'Mapel dihapus!') : set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menghapus.');
    }
    header("Location: data_mapel.php");
    exit();
}

<file path="includes/helper_fungsi.php">
<?php
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

/**
 * Membersihkan input form dari karakter berbahaya (Anti-XSS & Trim)
 */
function sanitize($data) {
    if (is_null($data)) return '';
    $data = trim($data);
    $data = stripslashes($data);
    return htmlspecialchars($data, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
}

/**
 * Menyiapkan pesan notifikasi temporary (flash message)
 * $tipe: 'success', 'danger', 'warning', 'info'
 */
function set_flash_message($tipe, $pesan) {
    $_SESSION['flash_message'] = [
        'tipe' => $tipe,
        'pesan' => $pesan
    ];
}

/**
 * Menampilkan pesan notifikasi dalam bentuk Alert Bootstrap 5
 */
function display_flash_message() {
    if (!isset($_SESSION['flash_message']) || !is_array($_SESSION['flash_message'])) {
        return;
    }

    $flash = $_SESSION['flash_message'];
    unset($_SESSION['flash_message']); // Flash message hanya ditampilkan satu kali.

    $allowed_types = ['success', 'danger', 'warning', 'info'];
    $type = in_array($flash['tipe'] ?? '', $allowed_types, true)
        ? $flash['tipe']
        : 'info';
    $message = htmlspecialchars((string) ($flash['pesan'] ?? ''), ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');

    $icons = [
        'success' => 'bi-check-circle-fill',
        'danger'  => 'bi-exclamation-triangle-fill',
        'warning' => 'bi-exclamation-circle-fill',
        'info'    => 'bi-info-circle-fill',
    ];

    echo '<div class="alert alert-' . $type . ' sipn-flash-alert" role="status" aria-live="polite">';
    echo '<span class="sipn-flash-alert__content">';
    echo '<i class="bi ' . $icons[$type] . '" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
    echo '<span>' . $message . '</span>';
    echo '</span>';
    echo '<button type="button" class="btn-close" aria-label="Tutup notifikasi" onclick="this.parentNode.remove()">&times;</button>';
    echo '</div>';
}
?>
</file>

                </div>
                <div class="footer-badges">

<file path="includes/helper_cek_login.php">
<?php
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/helper_fungsi.php';

/**
 * Memastikan pengguna sudah melakukan login
 */
function check_login() {
    if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
        set_flash_message('warning', 'Silakan login terlebih dahulu untuk mengakses halaman ini.');
        header("Location: " . BASE_URL . "auth/form_login.php");
        exit();
    }
}

/**
 * Memastikan role pengguna sesuai dengan halaman yang diakses
 * Contoh penggunaan: check_role(['admin']);
 */
function check_role($allowed_roles = []) {
    check_login();
    
    if (!in_array($_SESSION['role'], $allowed_roles)) {
        $role_sekarang = $_SESSION['role'];
        set_flash_message('danger', 'Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk membuka halaman tersebut.');
        header("Location: " . BASE_URL . $role_sekarang . "/dashboard_" . $role_sekarang . ".php");
        exit();
    }
}
?>
</file>
